[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

A fireplace sits empty, piles of ash sit in the base, as if it was abandoned while a fire was still burning.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, north

[Dremsyl's Tower, Resident's Sleeping Quarters]
Moth-eaten curtains hang from the ceiling around each bed, likely designed to give residents the semblance of privacy. The mattresses lie unused, stripped of their linens and gathering dust. Some of the beds have crumbled to the ground, their wood rotten or eaten through by insects. Dripping fluids from the creature above fall onto the uncovered mattresses and begin to smoke as it slowly eats away at the fabric and feathers.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Resident's Bathing Room]

A tiled mosaic runs across the walls of the room, the design is impossible to comprehend behind layers of grime and creeping fleshy tentacles. A faintly glowing orb sits on a wrought golden stand just inside the archway of the room.

The area is illuminated.

Large armoires line the walls, giving the resident magi a place to keep clothes and personal items. [Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

Thick veins of glowing red and blue run through the creature's blubbery flesh, giving off enough light to illuminate the room.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Armory]
que clear
que clear
A bright glowing blue barricade protects this room from the massive flesh monster's intrusion, keeping the powerful magical artifacts stored here safe from its corrupting touch. Weapon stands hold potent magical staves and rare wands glimmer with runic energy from velvet cushioned glass cases. The room shudders in time with a deafening thumping coming from the flesh monster. It pulses violently with each thump, and on closer inspection, the cause is revealed. A colossal heart pumps powerfully, sending gallons of thick, sap-like blood rushing through the veins of the monster. The glow coming from the creature is bright, almost blindingly so, and the force with which the creature pumps its heart causes the blood to churn fiercely, turning the normally dancing red and blue lights into a searing purple radiance.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

The flesh of the massive creature taking over the tower has grown thick here, and the blood rushes through the veins just beneath the flesh so quickly that the individual blue and red glow has blurred, casting a purple luminescence across the room. Large tentacles wave threateningly through the room, as if they seek to stop intruders from venturing further to the east.

The area is illuminated.

The nests are small and tucked into the crevices in the massive wooden beams along the ceiling, no doubt once the homes of the Zacotl that served the master of the tower, and oversaw the Goblins. A thick layer of rope-like tentacles dangle from the ceiling, blocking the view to the south, as if they seek to keep something hidden.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Salevos the Aka'thri,  Geza the Urgon,  Sythast the Urgon
Obvious exits are: west, east

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A large rat scuttles across the floor, exploring the space for food. Its tiny paws make a soft scratching sound across the stone floor as it hurries by. A strange bulbous protrusion in the meaty flesh of the tower creature shifts as it hears the rat approach. With a nauseating squelching sound, the protrusion opens, revealing a single, massive blood-shot eyeball. The orb rotates in its socket, until it locates the unwary rodent. The beast pulses and a rubbery tentacle shoots out at the rat as a gaping mouth appears on the end. In an instant the rat is gone, and the eye closes again.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
To the south is what is left of the dormitories of the mages and alchemists that once populated the tower. A soft thumping sound is coming from further down the hallway to the east. [Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Landing]
The nauseating, sweet scent of death fills the air thickly. Coppery scented blood drips from the ceiling in a constant flow, pooling into a depression on the rocky floor. Above, the source of the streaming blood is an immense pulsing blob of shapeless, living flesh. It extends across the entire ceiling, and far into the next rooms. Thick veins of glowing red and blue run through the creature's blubbery flesh, giving off enough light to illuminate the room.

The area is illuminated.

[Dremsyl's Tower, Second Floor Hallway]
A weak breeze of fresh air blows through the area from a thin window along the far west wall. The swamp air cuts through the cloying scent of death, giving a momentary relief from the oppressive weight of the stale tower air. Thin, hair-like tentacles of the creature growing in the tower reach through the window, as if seeking to spread past the confines of the looming stone structure, to infect the rest of the swampland below.
